```
def _proj_box(x,S):
  x=np.clip(x,0,1).copy()
  d=S-x.sum()
  for _ in range(50):
    if abs(d)<1e-14: break
    if d>0: mask=x<1.0; c=mask.sum()
    else: mask=x>0; c=mask.sum()
    if c==0: break
    x[mask]+=d/c
  return x
```

Now, FISTA details with sym projection and P-only gradient:
```
def _fista_sym(h0, a, t_end, L0=20.0):
    h=_sym_repair(h0)
    n=h.size; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=_c5_full(h)
    hp=h.copy(); y=h.copy(); t=1.0
    fy,_=_soft_valgrad(h,a)
    L=L0
    stale=0
    for it in range(100000):
      if time.time()>=t_end: break
      if it:
        tn=0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t)); y=h+((t-1)/tn)*(h-hp); t=tn
        y=_sym_repair(y)
        fy,_=_soft_valgrad(y,a)
      else: y=h; fy=fh
      g=_soft_grad(y,a)
      step=1/max(L,1e-8)
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(12):
        xn=_sym_repair(y-step*g)
        fx,_=_soft_valgrad(xn,a)
        dv=xn-y
        rhs=fy+dot(g,dv)+0.5/step*dot(dv,dv)
        if fx<=rhs+1e-10: accepted=True; break
        step*=0.5
      L=1/step
      if accepted:
        hp=h; h=xn; stale=0
        # update L adapt maybe
        L=max(L*0.7,1.0)
        if it%20==0: c=_c5_full(h); if c<best: update
      else:
        stale+=1; y=h; t=1; fy,fh? Need store fx last? We didn't set. Set fy=_soft_valgrad(h,a)[0]
        L=max(L*0.5,1.0); if stale>15: break
    final update
```
Need careful variable names. Use t_end absolute time to manage phases.

    step=1e-3 maybe

```
import numpy as np
import time
# maybe scipy not needed
def _fft_size(n):...
def _proj_box(x,S):...
def _sym_make(x):...
def _sym_repair(h):...
def _pn_pos(h):...
def _pn_full(h):... # optional
def _c5_full(h): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'))*2/len(h))
```

`_grad_weights_P(h,wP)`:
```
def _grad_weights_P(h,wP):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    lin=np.empty(n); lin[0]=0? Actually wP[0]*(1-2h) separately.
    u=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1:
       u[1:]=wP[1:]
       cp=np.zeros(n); cp[1:]=np.cumsum(wP[1:])
       linP=cp[n-1::-1]
    else: linP=np.zeros(n)
    q=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1 and np.any(u[1:]):
       Nf=_fft_size(n); H=np.fft.rfft(h,Nf)
       R=np.fft.irfft(H*np.fft.rfft(u[::-1],Nf),Nf)
       L=np.fft.irfft(H*np.fft.rfft(u,Nf),Nf)
       q=-(R[n-1:2*n-1].real + L[:n].real)
    return dx*(wP[0]*(1-2h)+linP+q)
```

def _local_search(h0, deadline, rng, base0=0.02):
  h=_sym_repair(h0); n,m; c=_c5_full(h); best...
  P,mx=_pn_pos(h); gx,_=...
  order=np.argsort(gx); q=max(8,m//20); low=order[:q]; high=order[-q:]
  base=base0
  stall=0; recompute=0; t0=time.time(); total=deadline-t0
  while time<deadline:
    if recompute or it%1000==0: recompute grad/labelling; if c<best update; if stall>5000: base*=0.7; stall=0
    frac=min(1,(time-t0)/max(total,1)); base = max(2e-5, base0*(1e-5)**frac*0.5?)
    # choose pair
    if rng.random()<0.75:
       source=high[rand]; sink=low[rand]
    else: random pair
    if same continue
    # ensure source has higher gx if random: if gx[source] < gx[sink]: swap
    maxd=min(h[source],1-h[sink])
    if maxd <=1e-12: continue
    d=base*(0.3+1.7*rng.random()); d=min(d,maxd)
    best_local=d; best_c=c

    candidates = unique/filter [d,0.5,1.5,0.2,3]
    for f in candidates:
       dd=min(maxd, d*f)
       if dd<1e-12 continue
       apply; c2; if c2<best_c: best_c=c2; best_local=dd; if c2<c accept? We can just after loop apply best if improved.

Implement `_pn_pos`:
```
def _pn_pos(h):
 n=len(h); N=_fft_size(n); H=np.fft.rfft(h,N); ac=np.fft.irfft(H*np.conj(H),N).real[:n]
 pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(h,out=pref[1:]); total=pref[n]
 P=np.empty(n); P[0]=total-ac[0]
 if n>1:
   idx=np.arange(1,n)
   P[idx]=pref[n-idx]-ac[idx]
 mx=P.max()
 return P,mx
```
This is P for k=0..n-1. For full positive correlation, `np.correlate(h,1-h,full)` includes lags maybe reverse order; But P for k up to n-1. exact c5 via correlate safer.

`_grad_weights_P`: Need _fft_size. The q formula: uses `u[::-1]` and `L[:n]`. Let's test for P only. Might be okay from previous. If any issue, local search may choose bad direction. We can instead compute gradient via finite differences for active set occasionally? Too slow. Or compute gradient directly O(n^2) since n=512 and recompute every 1000 moves; O(n^2)=262k, okay. We can implement direct gradient for active P to avoid FFT formula bugs.

Direct gradient of P_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_i - ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_i h_{i+k}. For each k active weight w[k]. Gradient dP/dh_j:
- linear: sum_{k: 0 ≤ j ≤ n-k-1} w_k = sum_{k: 1 ≤ k ≤ n-j} w_k for j? Condition j < n-k => k < n-j. k from 1 to n-j-1? For j=0, k=1..n-1; j=n-1, none. So linear[j]=sum_{k=1}^{n-1-j} w_k. This is suffix sum previous linP.
- derivative of 0 lag w0*(1-2h_j).
- quadratic: - for pairs (i,i+k). For source j as first: -w_k h_j if j+k<n -> sum over k=1..n-1-j w_k h_{j+k}. For source as second: -w_k h_{j-k} if k≤j -> sum over k=1..j w_k h_{j-k}. Total quadr[j] = -[∑_{k=1}^{n-1-j} w_k h_{j+k} + ∑_{k=1}^{j} w_k h_{j-k}]. This can be computed via convolutions or O(n^2) directly with loops? n=512, active maybe few; we can loop active k and accumulate O(cnt*n). If active set small (maybe few), fast. We can compute active weights list, then:
```
g = wP[0]*(1-2h)
lin suffix O(n)
g += lin
for k,w in active:
   # for first positions i=0..n-k-1: derivative wrt h_i: -h_{i+k}; wrt h_{i+k}: -h_i
   g[:n-k] += -w * h[k:]
   g[k:] += -w * h[:n-k]
```
This is O(active*n). If active set uses tolerance, maybe 20 lags; 20*512=10k, very fast. Recompute every 1000 moves cheap. This direct gradient is simpler and exact for P active. Let's use it, avoiding FFT. It uses only P active lags (positive directions). For symmetric objective, good.

Compute active lags from P, mx, tol. `active_k` where P>=mx-tol. For each, weight=1/cnt. For k=0, linear derivative w0*(1-2h). For k>0: as above.
Then sym gradient full g = 0.5*(g+g[::-1]); gx = g[:m]+g[m:][::-1]. This should guide accurately.

Let's write `_local_search` carefully with time and rng.

Pseudo:
```
def _local_search(h0, deadline, rng, base0=0.02):
    h=_sym_repair(h0)
    n=len(h); m=n//2
    c=_c5_full(h); best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    if time.time() >= deadline: return h,best_c
    gx, mxP = _active_gx(h)
    order=np.argsort(gx)
    q=max(8, m//16)
    low=order[:q]; high=order[-q:]
    base=base0
    stall=0; attempts=0; last_recompute=0
    t0=time.time(); total=max(1.0, deadline-t0)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        attempts +=1
        if attempts == last_recompute or stall > 5000 or c < last_best_c - ...?
           gx,mxP=_active_gx(h); if c<best update; order=argsort; low/high; base=...; last_recompute=attempts+300
        frac=(time.time()-t0)/total
        base = max(2e-5, base0*(0.2**frac)? maybe decays slow)
        if stall > 10000:
            base = min(0.05, base*0.5) ; stall=0
        # choose
        if rng.random()<0.8:
           src=int(high[rng.randint(high.size)]); snk=int(low[rng.randint(low.size)])
        else:
           src=int(rng.randint(m)); snk=int(rng.randint(m))
        if src==snk: continue
        if gx[src] < gx[snk]: swap  # ensure src high
        maxd = min(h[src], 1-h[snk])
        if maxd < 1e-12: continue
        d = base*(0.2+1.8*rng.random())
        if d > maxd: d=maxd
        # candidates multipliers
        multipliers = (0.5,1.0,2.0)
        best_d=0; best_c=c
        mi=n-1-src; mj=n-1-snk
        old = (h[src], h[mi], h[snk], h[mj])
        for mult in multipliers:
            dd = min(maxd, d*mult)
            if dd < 1e-13: continue
            h[src]=old[0]-dd; h[mi]=old[1]-dd; h[snk]=old[2]+dd; h[mj]=old[3]+dd
            c2=_c5_full(h)
            if c2 < best_c:
               best_c=c2; best_d=dd
            # if mult==? maybe early if c2<=c? no
        # restore
        h[src]=old[0]; h[mi]=old[1]; h[snk]=old[2]; h[mj]=old[3]
        if best_d > 0 and best_c < c - 1e-15:
            h[src]=old[0]-best_d; h[mi]=old[1]-best_d; h[snk]=old[2]+best_d; h[mj]=old[3]+best_d
            c=best_c
            stall=0
            if c < best_c - 1e-15: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
        else:
            stall +=1
            if stall > 20000: # restart from best_h? maybe perturb
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = best_c
                stall=0
        # optional if time check continue
    h=_sym_repair(h); c=_c5_full(h); if c<best... return best_h,best_c
```

def _piece_p_sample(n,p):
   y = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)-1.0
   ay=np.abs(y)
   if p<=1e-12: u=1-2*ay
   else:
      a=(1-p)/(1+p); slope=-2/(1-p*p)
      u=np.where(ay<=p, a, a+slope*(ay-p))
   h=0.5+0.5*np.clip(u,-1,1)
   return _sym_repair(h)
```

Now, `_sym_repair`: use _proj_box on half. `_proj_box` may produce values slightly outside due iterative; after project, clip again:
```
def _proj_box(x,S):
    x=np.array(x,dtype=float,copy=True)
    for _ in range(50):
       np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
       d=S-x.sum()
       if abs(d)<1e-13: break
       if d>0: mask=x<1; c=mask.sum()
       else: mask=x>0; c=mask.sum()
       if c==0: break
       x[mask]+=d/c
    # final exact distribute small d
    d=S-x.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-15:
       if d>0: order=np.argsort(1-x)
       else: order=np.argsort(x)
       acc=0
       for idx in order:
          if d>0: add=min(d,1-x[idx]) else add=max(d,-x[idx])
          x[idx]+=add; d-=add
          if abs(d)<1e-15: break
    return x
```
This final loop can be slow if d not found? d small; okay. For exact sum maybe. But if d clips break earlier.

`_c5_full`:
```
def _c5_full(h):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/len(h)))
```

def _resample_sym(h,m):
 n=len(h); mm=m//2
 x=0.5*(h[:n//2]+h[n//2:][::-1])
 x0=(np.arange(n//2)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
 x1=(np.arange(mm)+0.5)*(2.0/m)
 xx=np.interp(x1,x0,x)
 xx=_proj_box(xx, mm*0.5)
 return _sym_make(xx)
```

```
def _local_search(h0, deadline, rng, base0=0.02):
  h=_sym_repair(h0); n=len(h); m=n//2
  cur_c=_c5_full(h); best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
  if time.time()>=deadline: return best_h,best_c
  gx,_=_active_gx_sym(h)
  order=np.argsort(gx); q=max(8,m//12); low=order[:q]; high=order[-q:]
  base=base0; since=0; stall=0; min_base=2e-5
  t0=time.time(); total=max(1.0, deadline-t0)
  while time.time()<deadline:
    if since>400 or stall>3000:
       gx,mx=_active_gx_sym(h); if cur_c<best_c: ...
       order=np.argsort(gx); low=order[:q]; high=order[-q:]
       since=0
    frac=min(1.0, (time.time()-t0)/total)
    base = max(min_base, base0*(0.15**frac))  # decays from base0 to 3e-3? 0.15^1=0.015*0.02=0.0003
    if stall > 8000:
       base = max(min_base, base*0.5)
       if rng.random()<0.5: base = min(0.05, base*4)
    if rng.random()<0.75:
       src=int(high[rng.randint(high.size)]); snk=int(low[rng.randint(low.size)])
    else:
       src=int(rng.randint(m)); snk=int(rng.randint(m))
    if src==snk: continue
    if gx[src] < gx[snk]: src,snk=snk,src
    maxd=float(min(h[src], 1-h[snk]))
    if maxd <=1e-12: continue
    since+=1
    d=base*(0.2+1.8*rng.random())
    if rng.random()<0.02: d*=5.0
    if d>maxd: d=maxd
    mi=n-1-src; mj=n-1-snk
    old_src=h[src]; old_snk=h[snk]
    best_d=0.0; best_cc=cur_c
    for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       dd=d*mult
       if dd>maxd: dd=maxd
       if dd<1e-13: continue
       h[src]=old_src-dd; h[mi]=old_src-dd
       h[snk]=old_snk+dd; h[mj]=old_snk+dd
       c2=_c5_full(h)
       if c2<best_cc: best_cc=c2; best_d=dd
       if c2 >= cur_c-1e-15 and mult==2.0: # optional break? no
          pass
    h[src]=old_src; h[mi]=old_src; h[snk]=old_snk; h[mj]=old_snk
    if best_d > 0 and best_cc < cur_c - 1e-15:
       h[src]=old_src-best_d; h[mi]=old_src-best_d; h[snk]=old_snk+best_d; h[mj]=old_snk+best_d
       cur_c=best_cc; stall=0
       if best_cc < best_c - 1e-15: best_c=best_cc; best_h=h.copy()
       base = max(min_base, min(0.04, 0.8*base + 0.2*best_d))
    else:
       stall += 1
       if stall > 20000:
          h=best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c; stall=0
          since=0
    if time.time()>=deadline: break
  # final if cur_c < best_c? update
  h=_sym_repair(h); c=_c5_full(h); if c<best_c: ...
  return best_h,best_c
```

Let's code `_pn_pos` carefully:
```
def _pn_pos(h):
    n = len(h)
    N = 1
    m = 2*n - 1
    while N < m: N <<= 1
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), N).real[:n]
    pref = np.empty(n+1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    total = pref[n]
    P = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    P[0] = total - ac[0]
    if n > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1,n)
        P[idx] = pref[n-idx] - ac[idx]
    return P, float(P.max())
```
The `ac` from autocorr of h has small imaginary? irfft real. Good.

```
active = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
w = np.zeros(n); w[active] = 1.0/cnt
g = np.zeros(n)
if w[0]: g += w[0]*(1-2*h)
if n>1 and np.any(w[1:]):
   a = w[1:]
   suff = np.cumsum(a[::-1])[::-1]
   g[:n-1] += suff
   ks = np.nonzero(w[1:])[0] + 1
   for k in ks:
      wk = w[k]
      seg = n-k
      g[:seg] -= wk * h[k:]
      g[k:] -= wk * h[:seg]
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _proj_box(x,S): ...
def _sym_make(x): ...
def _sym_repair(h): ...
def _resample_sym(h,m): ...
def _c5_full(h): ...
def _pn_pos(h): ...
def _active_gx_sym(h): ...
def _local_search(...): ...
def _piece_p_sample(n,p): ...
def _power_sample(n,q): ...
def run(...): ...
```

In `_proj_box`, using `for _ in range(80)`. Need dtype float. After final, `np.clip` again? If final distribute may push slightly beyond due clip? We can clip and then fix sum with tiny distribution. Simpler:
```
def _proj_box(x,S):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    for _ in range(60):
       np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
       d=S-x.sum()
       if abs(d)<1e-13: break
       if d>0: mask=x<1.0
       else: mask=x>0.0
       c=int(mask.sum())
       if c==0: break
       x[mask] += d/c
    d=S-x.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-15:
       order=np.argsort(1-x if d>0 else x)
       for idx in order:
          if d>0:
             add=min(d,1.0-x[idx])
          else:
             add=max(d,-x[idx])
          x[idx]+=add
          d-=add
          if abs(d)<1e-15: break
    return x
```
If after distributing, maybe sum exact but some x slightly out due floating? We can final `np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)`. But clipping changes sum tiny. Good enough. Use clipping before final? Already.

`_sym_make`: If x length m, `out = np.empty(2*m); out[:m]=x; out[m:]=x[::-1]; return out`.

`_sym_repair`: ensure h length even. Use `x=0.5*(h[:m]+h[m:][::-1])`. If h not even? assume even.

`_resample_sym`: if len(h)==m, return _sym_repair. Else use interp.

`_piece_p_sample`: Need `p = float(min(max(p,0.0),0.9))`. If p=0, use u=1-2ay. Else a=(1-p)/(1+p), slope=-2/(1-p*p). For `ay<=p`, use a; else a+slope*(ay-p). Since p maybe 0, slope large. Clip.

`_power_sample`: y=(centers)-1; ay. if q<=1: u=1-(q+1)*ay**q. Else u=1/(q+1)-ay**q. For q=1 exactly branch q<=1 gives tent. Bring. h=0.5+0.5*clip. Project.

```
def _local_search(h0, deadline, rng, base0=0.02):
    h = _sym_repair(h0)
    n = len(h); m = n//2
    cur_c = _c5_full(h)
    best_c = cur_c; best_h = h.copy()
    if time.time() >= deadline: return best_h,best_c
    gx, _ = _active_gx_sym(h)
    order = np.argsort(gx)
    q = max(8, m//12)
    low = order[:q]; high = order[-q:]
    base = base0; it=0; stall=0; since=0
    t0=time.time(); total=max(1.0, deadline-t0)
    while time.time() < deadline:
       it +=1
       if it % 2000 ==0:
          h = _sym_repair(h); cur_c = _c5_full(h)
          if cur_c < best_c: best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy()
       if since > 400 or stall > 2000:
          gx, _ = _active_gx_sym(h)
          order=np.argsort(gx); low=order[:q]; high=order[-q:]; since=0
       frac = min(1.0, (time.time()-t0)/total)
       base = max(5e-5, base0*(0.1**frac))
       if stall > 4000:
          base = min(0.05, base*2.0)
          stall = 0
       elif stall > 8000:
          base = max(5e-5, base*0.5)
          stall = 0
       if rng.random() < 0.75:
          src = int(high[rng.randint(high.size)])
          snk = int(low[rng.randint(low.size)])
       else:
          src = int(rng.randint(m)); snk=int(rng.randint(m))
       src, snk, high? if same continue.
       if src == snk: continue
       if gx[src] < gx[snk]: src,snk=snk,src
       mi=n-1-src; mj=n-1-snk
       o=(float(h[src]),float(h[mi]),float(h[snk]),float(h[mj]))
       maxd = min(o[0],o[1],1.0-o[2],1.0-o[3])
       if maxd <= 1e-12: continue
       since +=1
       d = base*(0.25+1.5*rng.random())
       if rng.random() < 0.03: d *= 4.0
       if d > maxd: d = maxd
       best_d = 0.0; best_cc = cur_c
       for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
          dd = d*mult
          if dd > maxd: dd=maxd
          if dd < 1e-13: continue
          h[src]=o[0]-dd; h[mi]=o[1]-dd
          h[snk]=o[2]+dd; h[mj]=o[3]+dd
          c2 = _c5_full(h)
          if c2 < best_cc:
             best_cc = c2; best_d = dd
       h[src]=o[0]; h[mi]=o[1]; h[snk]=o[2]; h[mj]=o[3]
       accepted=False
       if best_d > 0.0:
          if best_cc < cur_c - 1e-15:
             accepted=True
          elif stall > 3000 and best_cc < cur_c + 1e-3:
             T = 1e-4*max(1e-3, 1.0-frac)
             if rng.random() < np.exp(-(best_cc-cur_c)/T): accepted=True
       if accepted:
          h[src]=o[0]-best_d; h[mi]=o[1]-best_d
          h[snk]=o[2]+best_d; h[mj]=o[3]+best_d
          cur_c = best_cc; stall=0
          if cur_c < best_c - 1e-15:
             best_c = cur_c; best_h = h.copy()
          base = max(5e-5, min(0.04, 0.8*base + 0.2*best_d))
       else:
          stall +=1
          if stall > 20000:
             h = best_h.copy(); cur_c=best_c; stall=0; since=1000
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
    h = _sym_repair(h)
    c = _c5_full(h)
    if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    return best_h,best_c
```
If accepts worse, best_h not updated; cur_c worse. Later if improves below best, best_h h (which is worse at time but after improvement maybe below). Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def _proj_box(x, S):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    for _ in range(80):
        np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
        d = S - x.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0.0:
            mask = x < 1.0
        else:
            mask = x > 0.0
        c = int(mask.sum())
        if c == 0:
            break
        x[mask] += d / c
    d = S - x.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-15:
        if d > 0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - x)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(x)
        for idx in order:
            if d > 0:
                a = min(d, 1.0 - x[idx])
            else:
                a = max(d, -x[idx])
            x[idx] += a
            d -= a
            if abs(d) < 1e-15:
                break
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    return x


def _sym_make(x):
    m = len(x)
    out = np.empty(2 * m, dtype=float)
    out[:m] = x
    out[m:] = x[::-1]
    return out


def _sym_repair(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    m = n // 2
    x = 0.5 * (h[:m] + h[m:][::-1])
    x = _proj_box(x, 0.5 * m)
    return _sym_make(x)


def _resample_sym(h, m):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == m:
        return _sym_repair(h)
    nm = n // 2
    mm = m // 2
    x = 0.5 * (h[:nm] + h[nm:][::-1])
    x0 = (np.arange(nm) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x1 = (np.arange(mm) + 0.5) * (2.0 / m)
    xx = np.interp(x1, x0, x)
    xx = _proj_box(xx, 0.5 * mm)
    return _sym_make(xx)


def _c5_full(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))


def _pn_pos(h):
    n = len(h)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(H * np.conj(H), N).real[:n]
    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    total = pref[n]
    P = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    P[0] = total - ac[0]
    if n > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1, n)
        P[idx] = pref[n - idx] - ac[idx]
    return P, float(P.max())


def _active_gx_sym(h):
    n = len(h)
    P, mx = _pn_pos(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    tol = max(1e-8, 10.0 * dx)
    mask = P >= mx - tol
    cnt = int(mask.sum())
    if cnt == 0:
        m = n // 2
        return np.zeros(m), mx * dx

    w = np.zeros(n)
    w[mask] = 1.0 / cnt
    g = np.zeros(n)

    if w[0] > 0.0:
        g += w[0] * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    if n > 1 and np.any(w[1:]):
        a = w[1:]
        suff = np.cumsum(a[::-1])[::-1]
        g[:n - 1] += suff

        ks = np.nonzero(w[1:])[0] + 1
        for k in ks:
            k = int(k)
            wk = w[k]
            seg = n - k
            g[:seg] -= wk * h[k:]
            g[k:] -= wk * h[:seg]

    g = 0.5 * (g + g[::-1])
    m = n // 2
    gx = g[:m] + g[m:][::-1]
    return gx, mx * dx


def _local_search(h0, deadline, rng, base0=0.02):
    h = _sym_repair(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float))
    n = len(h)
    m = n // 2
    cur_c = _c5_full(h)
    best_c = cur_c
    best_h = h.copy()

    if time.time() >= deadline:
        return best_h, best_c

    gx, _ = _active_gx_sym(h)
    order = np.argsort(gx)
    q = max(8, m // 12)
    low = order[:q]
    high = order[-q:]

    base = base0
    it = 0
    since = 0
    stall = 0
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(1.0, deadline - t0)

    while time.time() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            h = _sym_repair(h)
            cur_c = _c5_full(h)
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c = cur_c
                best_h = h.copy()

        if since > 400 or stall > 2000:
            gx, _ = _active_gx_sym(h)
            order = np.argsort(gx)
            low = order[:q]
            high = order[-q:]
            since = 0

        frac = min(1.0, (time.time() - t0) / total)
        floor = max(5e-5, base0 * (0.1 ** frac))
        if base < floor:
            base = floor
        if base > 0.06:
            base = 0.06

        if stall > 4000:
            base = min(0.06, base * 2.0)
            stall = 0
        elif stall > 8000:
            base = max(5e-5, base * 0.5)
            stall = 0

        if rng.random() < 0.75:
            src = int(high[rng.randint(high.size)])
            snk = int(low[rng.randint(low.size)])
        else:
            src = int(rng.randint(m))
            snk = int(rng.randint(m))

        if src == snk:
            continue

        if gx[src] < gx[snk]:
            src, snk = snk, src

        mi = n - 1 - src
        mj = n - 1 - snk
        o = (float(h[src]), float(h[mi]), float(h[snk]), float(h[mj]))
        maxd = min(o[0], o[1], 1.0 - o[2], 1.0 - o[3])
        if maxd <= 1e-12:
            continue

        since += 1
        d = base * (0.25 + 1.5 * rng.random())
        if rng.random() < 0.03:
            d *= 4.0
        if d > maxd:
            d = maxd

        best_d = 0.0
        best_cc = cur_c

        for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            dd = d * mult
            if dd > maxd:
                dd = maxd
            if dd < 1e-13:
                continue
            h[src] = o[0] - dd
            h[mi] = o[1] - dd
            h[snk] = o[2] + dd
            h[mj] = o[3] + dd
            c2 = _c5_full(h)
            if c2 < best_cc:
                best_cc = c2
                best_d = dd

        h[src] = o[0]
        h[mi] = o[1]
        h[snk] = o[2]
        h[mj] = o[3]

        accepted = False
        if best_d > 0.0:
            if best_cc < cur_c - 1e-15:
                accepted = True
            elif stall > 3000 and best_cc < cur_c + 1e-3:
                T = 1e-4 * max(1e-3, 1.0 - frac)
                if rng.random() < np.exp(-(best_cc - cur_c) / T):
                    accepted = True

        if accepted:
            h[src] = o[0] - best_d
            h[mi] = o[1] - best_d
            h[snk] = o[2] + best_d
            h[mj] = o[3] + best_d
            cur_c = best_cc
            stall = 0
            if cur_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = cur_c
                best_h = h.copy()
            base = max(5e-5, min(0.04, 0.8 * base + 0.2 * best_d))
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 20000:
                h = best_h.copy()
                cur_c = best_c
                stall = 0
                since = 1000

        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break

    h = _sym_repair(h)
    c = _c5_full(h)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h
    return best_h, best_c


def _piece_p_sample(n, p):
    p = float(min(max(p, 0.0), 0.85))
    y = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n) - 1.0
    ay = np.abs(y)
    if p <= 1e-12:
        u = 1.0 - 2.0 * ay
    else:
        a = (1.0 - p) / (1.0 + p)
        slope = -2.0 / (1.0 - p * p)
        u = np.where(ay <= p, a, a + slope * (ay - p))
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)
    return _sym_repair(h)


def _power_sample(n, q):
    y = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n) - 1.0
    ay = np.abs(y)
    q = float(q)
    if q <= 1.0:
        u = 1.0 - (q + 1.0) * np.power(ay, q)
    else:
        u = (1.0 / (q + 1.0)) - np.power(ay, q)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)
    return _sym_repair(h)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    N = 512

    best_h = _piece_p_sample(N, 0.0)
    best_c = _c5_full(best_h)

    try:
        seeds = []

        if "initial_h_values" in globals():
            hp = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
            if len(hp) > 0:
                seeds.append(_resample_sym(hp, N))

        for p in (0.0, 0.07, 0.14, 0.21, 0.28, 0.35, 0.42):
            seeds.append(_piece_p_sample(N, p))

        for q in (0.65, 0.85, 1.25, 1.65, 2.4):
            seeds.append(_power_sample(N, q))

        scored = []
        for idx, sh in enumerate(seeds):
            sh = _sym_repair(sh)
            seeds[idx] = sh
            scored.append((_c5_full(sh), idx))
        scored.sort()

        if scored:
            c0, i0 = scored[0]
            if c0 < best_c:
                best_c = c0
                best_h = seeds[i0].copy()

        end_local = t0 + budget_s - 25.0
        usable0 = max(1.0, end_local - time.time())

        fracs = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]
        top = min(3, len(seeds))
        slots = [fracs[i] * usable0 for i in range(top)]

        for rank in range(top):
            if time.time() >= end_local - 5.0:
                break
            slot = min(slots[rank], end_local - time.time())
            if slot < 8.0:
                continue
            deadline = time.time() + slot
            if deadline > end_local:
                deadline = end_local
            h2, c2 = _local_search(seeds[scored[rank][1]], deadline, rng, base0=0.02)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()
            if time.time() >= end_local:
                break

        end_polish = t0 + budget_s - 8.0
        if time.time() < end_polish - 5.0:
            deadline = end_polish
            h2, c2 = _local_search(best_h, deadline, rng, base0=0.005)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()

        u = 2.0 * best_h - 1.0
        for s in (0.88, 0.93, 0.97, 1.0, 1.03, 1.07, 1.12, 1.18):
            h2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.clip(u * s, -1.0, 1.0)
            h2 = _sym_repair(h2)
            c2 = _c5_full(h2)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()

        h2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(u)
        hmax = np.tanh(u).max()
        if hmax > 1e-8:
            h2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * (np.tanh(u) * (1.0 / hmax))
        h2 = _sym_repair(h2)
        c2 = _c5_full(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

        best_h = _sym_repair(best_h)
        best_c = _c5_full(best_h)

    except Exception:
        if len(best_h) != N:
            best_h = _sym_repair(best_h)
        else:
            best_h = _sym_repair(best_h)
        best_c = _c5_full(best_h)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
